NOTICE OF AGENCY ACTION Notice is hereby provided that the South Florida Water Management District, on July 30, 2026, issued an Environmental Resource Permit (ERP) (Permit Number 48-112453-P, Application Number 241217-48582) with conditions to the Diocese of Orlando, 50 E Robinson St, Orlando, FL 32801 for the construction and operation of a stormwater management (SWM) system serving 61.49 acres of residential development known as Southern Oaks Roadway & Mass Grading. The project proposes mass grading of the project site and construction of the master SWM system and includes construction of a roadway to connect Clapp Simms Duda Rd to Narcoossee Rd through the site located at 14901 Narcoossee Rd, Orlando, FL 32827 in Orange County Section 32, Township 24, and Range 31. A copy of the permit can be obtained by contacting the Regulatory Records Management Section, during normal business hours, 8:00 a.m. to 5:00 p.m., Monday through Friday, except legal holidays, at 3301 Gun Club Road, West Palm Beach, FL 33406, Regulation Division, by telephone at 561-682-6911, by e-mail at permits@sfwmd.gov, or by accessing the permit directly from the Districts website (www.sfwmd.gov) using the Application/Permit Search on the ePermitting page. The District's agency action is final unless a timely petition for an administrative hearing is filed under Sections 120.569 and 120.57 of the Florida Statutes before the deadline for filing a petition. As required by Sections 120.569 and 120.60(3), Fla. Stat., the following is notice of the opportunities which may be available for administrative hearing or judicial review when the substantial interests of a party are determined by an agency. Please note that this Notice of Rights is not intended to provide legal advice. Not all of the legal proceedings detailed below may be an applicable or appropriate remedy. You may wish to consult an attorney regarding your legal rights. RIGHT TO REQUEST ADMINISTRATIVE HEARING A person whose substantial interests are or may be affected by the South Florida Water Management Districts (SFWMD or District) action has the right to request an administrative hearing on that action pursuant to Sections 120.569 and 120.57, Fla. Stat. Persons seeking a hearing on a SFWMD decision which affects or may affect their substantial interests shall file a petition for hearing with the Office of the District Clerk of the SFWMD, in accordance with the filing instructions set forth herein, within 21 days of receipt of written notice of the decision, unless one of the following shorter time periods apply: (1) within 14 days of the notice of consolidated intent to grant or deny concurrently reviewed applications for environmental resource permits and use of sovereign submerged lands pursuant to Section 373.427, Fla. Stat.; or (2) within 14 days of service of an Administrative Order pursuant to Section 373.119(1), Fla. Stat. "Receipt of written notice of agency decision" means receipt of written notice through mail, electronic mail, or posting that the SFWMD has or intends to take final agency action, or publication of notice that the SFWMD has or intends to take final agency action. Any person who receives written notice of a SFWMD decision and fails to file a written request for hearing within the timeframe described above waives the right to request a hearing on that decision. If the District takes final agency action which materially differs from the noticed intended agency decision, persons who may be substantially affected shall, unless otherwise provided by law, have an additional Rule 28-106.111, Fla. Admin. Code, point of entry. Any person to whom an emergency order is directed pursuant to Section 373.119(2), Fla. Stat., shall comply therewith immediately, but on petition to the board shall be afforded a hearing as soon as possible. A person may file a request for an extension of time for filing a petition. The SFWMD may, for good cause, grant the request. Requests for extension of time must be filed with the SFWMD prior to the deadline for filing a petition for hearing. Such requests for extension shall contain a certificate that the moving party has consulted with all other parties concerning the extension and that the SFWMD and any other parties agree to or oppose the extension. A timely request for an extension of time shall toll the running of the time period for filing a petition until the request is acted upon. FILING INSTRUCTIONS A petition for administrative hearing must be filed with the Office of the District Clerk of the SFWMD. Filings with the Office of the District Clerk may be made by mail, hand-delivery, or e-mail. INITIATION OF AN ADMINISTRATIVE HEARING Pursuant to Sections 120.54(5)(b)4. and 120.569(2)(c), Fla. Stat., and Rules 28-106.201 and 28-106.301, Fla. Admin. Code, initiation of an administrative hearing shall be made by written petition to the SFWMD. MEDIATION The procedures for pursuing mediation are set forth in Section 120.573, Fla. Stat., and Rules 28-106.111 and 28-106.401.405, Fla. Admin. Code. The SFWMD is not proposing mediation for this agency action under Section 120.573, Fla. Stat., at this time. RIGHT TO SEEK JUDICIAL REVIEW Pursuant to Section 120.68, Fla. Stat., and in accordance with Florida Rule of Appellate Procedure 9.110, a party who is adversely affected by final SFWMD action may seek judicial review of the SFWMD's final decision by filing a notice of appeal with the Office of the District Clerk of the SFWMD in accordance with the filing instructions set forth herein within 30 days of rendition of the order to be reviewed, and by filing a copy of the notice with the clerk of the appropriate district court of appeal.

NOTICE OF RULE DEVELOPMENT BY THE LIVE OAK LAKE COMMUNITY DEVELOPMENT DISTRICT In accordance with Chapters 120 and 190, Florida Statutes, the Live Oak Lake Community Development District (District) hereby gives notice of its intention to develop revised Rules of Procedure to govern the operations of the District. The proposed rule number is Chapter 1. The revised Rules of Procedure will address such areas as the Board of Supervisors, officers and voting, district offices, public information and inspection of records, policies, public meetings, hearings and workshops, rulemaking proceedings, competitive purchase including procedure under the Consultants Competitive Negotiation Act, procedure regarding auditor selection, purchase of insurance, pre-qualification, construction contracts, goods, supplies and materials, maintenance services, contractual services and protests with respect to proceedings, as well as any other area of the general operation of the District. The purpose and effect of the revised Rules of Procedure is to provide for efficient and effective District operations and to ensure compliance with recent changes to Florida law. The specific grant of rulemaking authority for the adoption of the proposed revised Rules of Procedure includes Sections 190.011(5), 190.011(15) and 190.035, Florida Statutes. The specific laws implemented in the proposed revised Rules of Procedure include, but are not limited to, Sections 112.08, 112.3143, 112.31446, 112.3145, 119.07, 119.0701, 120.54, 120.542, 120.5435, 120.56, 120.69, 120.81, 189.053, 189.069, 190.006, 190.007, 190.008, 190.011, 190.033, 190.035, 218.33, 218.391, 255.05, 255.0518, 255.0525, 255.20, 286.0105, 286.011, 286.012, 286.0113, 286.0114, 287.017, 287.055, and 287.084, Florida Statutes. REQUEST FOR QUALIFICATIONS FOR ENGINEERING SERVICES FOR THE SOUTH VILLAGE NEW SMYRNA BEACH COMMUNITY DEVELOPMENT DISTRICT RFQ for Engineering Servi...

Details | Show full text
REQUEST FOR QUALIFICATIONS FOR ENGINEERING SERVICES FOR THE SOUTH VILLAGE NEW SMYRNA BEACH COMMUNITY DEVELOPMENT DISTRICT RFQ for Engineering Services The South Village New Smyrna Beach Community Development District (District), located in the City of New Smyrna Beach, Florida, announces that professional engineering services will be required on a continuing basis for the Districts public improvements authorized by Chapter 190, Florida Statutes. The engineering firm selected will act in the general capacity of District Engineer and provide District engineering services, as required. Any firm or individual (Applicant) desiring to provide professional services to the District must: 1) hold applicable federal, state and local licenses; 2) be authorized to do business in Florida in accordance with Florida law; and 3) furnish a statement (Qualification Statement) of its qualifications and past experience on U.S. General Service Administrations Architect-Engineer Qualifications, Standard Form No. 330, with pertinent supporting data. Among other things, Applicants must submit information relating to: a) the ability and adequacy of the Applicants professional personnel; b) whether the Applicant is a certified minority business enterprise; c) the Applicants willingness to meet time and budget requirements; d) the Applicants past experience and performance, including but not limited to past experience as a District Engineer for any community development districts and past experience with Polk County; e) the geographic location of the Applicants headquarters and offices; f) the current and projected workloads of the Applicant; and g) the volume of work previously awarded to the Applicant by the District. Further, each Applicant must identify the specific individual affiliated with the Applicant who would be handling District meetings, construction services, and other engineering tasks. The District will review all Applicants and will comply with Florida law, including the Consultants Competitive Negotiations Act, Chapter 287, Florida Statutes (CCNA). All applicants interested must submit one (1) original and one (1) copy of Standard Form No. 330 and Qualification Statement by 12:00 p.m. on Friday, September 4, 2026 to the attention of George Flint, GMS Central Florida, LLC, 219 East Livingston Street, Orlando, Florida 32801 (District Managers Office). The Board shall select and rank the Applicants using the requirements set forth in the CCNA and the evaluation criteria on file with the District Manager, and the highest ranked Applicant will be requested to enter into contract negotiations. If an agreement cannot be reached between the District and the highest ranked Applicant, negotiations will cease and begin with the next highest ranked Applicant, and if these negotiations are unsuccessful, will continue to the third highest ranked Applicant. The District reserves the right to reject any and all Qualification Statements. Additionally, there is no express or implied obligation for the District to reimburse Applicants for any expenses associated with the preparation and submittal of the Qualification Statements in response to this request. Any protest regarding the terms of this Notice, or the evaluation criteria on file with the District Manager, must be filed in writing, within seventy-two (72) calendar hours (excluding Saturdays, Sundays, and state holidays) after the publication of this Notice. The formal protest setting forth with particularity the facts and law upon which the protest is based shall be filed within seven (7) calendar days (including Saturdays, Sundays, and state holidays) after the initial notice of protest was filed. Failure to timely file a notice of protest or failure to timely file a formal written protest shall constitute a waiver of any right to object or protest with respect to aforesaid Notice or evaluation criteria provisions. Any person who files a notice of protest shall provide to the District, simultaneous with the filing of the notice, a protest bond with a responsible surety to be approved by the District and in the amount of Ten Thousand Dollars ($10,000.00). Additional information and requirements regarding protests are set forth in the Districts Rules of Procedure, which are available from the District Manager. George S. Flint District Manager Governmental Management Services Central Florida, LLC 114096 8/10/2026

NOTICE OF RULE DEVELOPMENT BY THE SOUTH VILLAGE NEW SMYRNA BEACH COMMUNITY DEVELOPMENT DISTRICT In accordance with Chapters 120 and 190, Florida Statutes, the South Village New Smyrna Beach Community Development District (District) hereby gives notice of its intention to develop Rules of Procedure to govern the operations of the District. The proposed rule number is Chapter 1. The Rules of Procedure will address such areas as the Board of Supervisors, officers and voting, district offices, public information and inspection of records, policies, public meetings, hearings and workshops, rulemaking proceedings, competitive purchase, including procedure under the Consultants Competitive Negotiation Act, procedure regarding auditor selection, purchase of insurance, pre-qualification, construction contracts, goods, supplies and materials, maintenance services, contractual services and protests with respect to proceedings, as well as any other area of the general operation of the District. The purpose and effect of the Rules of Procedure is to provide for efficient and effective District operations and to ensure compliance with recent changes to Florida law. The specific grant of rulemaking authority for the adoption of the proposed Rules of Procedure includes Sections 190.011(5), 190.011(15) and 190.035, Florida Statutes. The specific laws implemented in the proposed Rules of Procedure include, but are not limited to, Sections 112.08, 112.3143, 112.31446, 112.3145, 119.07, 119.0701, 120.54, 120.542, 120.5435, 120.56, 120.69, 120.81, 189.053, 189.069, 190.006, 190.007, 190.008, 190.011, 190.033, 190.035, 218.33, 218.391, 255.05, 255.0518, 255.0525, 255.20, 286.0105, 286.011, 286.012, 286.0113, 286.0114, 287.017, 287.055, and 287.084, Florida Statutes. A copy of the proposed Rules of Procedure and the related incorporated documents, if any, may be obtained by contacting the District Manager, c/o Governmental Management Services, 219 E. Livingston St., Orlando, Florida 32801, (407) 841-5524 (the District Managers Office). George Flint, District Manager South Village New Smyrna Beach Community Development District 114057 8/10/2026

REQUEST FOR QUALIFICATIONS FOR ENGINEERING SERVICES FOR THE HILLIARD ISLE COMMUNITY DEVELOPMENT DISTRICT RFQ for Engineering Services The Hilliard Isle Community Development District (District), located in the City of Kissimmee, Osceola County Florida, announces that professional engineering services will be required on a continuing basis for the Districts public improvements authorized by Chapter 190, Florida Statutes. The engineering firm selected will act in the general capacity of District Engineer and provide District engineering services, as required. Any firm or individual (Applicant) desiring to provide professional services to the District must: 1) hold applicable federal, state and local licenses; 2) be authorized to do business in Florida in accordance with Florida law; and 3) furnish a statement (Qualification Statement) of its qualifications and past experience on U.S. General Service Administrations Architect-Engineer Qualifications, Standard Form No. 330, with pertinent supporting data. Among other things, Applicants must submit information relating to: a) the ability and adequacy of the Applicants professional personnel; b) whether the Applicant is a certified minority business enterprise; c) the Applicants willingness to meet time and budget requirements; d) the Applicants past experience and performance, including but not limited to past experience as a District Engineer for any community development districts and past experience with Osceola County; e) the geographic location of the Applicants headquarters and offices; f) the current and projected workloads of the Applicant; and g) the volume of work previously awarded to the Applicant by the District. Further, each Applicant must identify the specific individual affiliated with the Applicant who would be handling District meetings, construction services, and other engineering tasks. The District will review all Applicants and will comply with Florida law, including the Consultants Competitive Negotiations Act, Chapter 287, Florida Statutes (CCNA). The District reserves the right to reject any and all Qualification Statements. Additionally, there is no express or implied obligation for the District to reimburse Applicants for any expenses associated with the preparation and submittal of the Qualification Statements in response to this request. Any protest regarding the terms of this Notice, or the evaluation criteria on file with the District Manager, must be filed in writing, within seventy-two (72) calendar hours (excluding Saturdays, Sundays, and state holidays) after the publication of this Notice. The formal protest setting forth with particularity the facts and law upon which the protest is based shall be filed within seven (7) calendar days (including Saturdays, Sundays, and state holidays) after the initial notice of protest was filed. Failure to timely file a notice of protest or failure to timely file a formal written protest shall constitute a waiver of any right to object or protest with respect to aforesaid Notice or evaluation criteria provisions. NOTICE OF RULE DEVELOPMENT BY THE OSCEOLA VILLAGE CENTER COMMUNITY DEVELOPMENT DISTRICT In accordance with Chapters 120 and 190, Florida Statutes, the Osceola Village Center Community Development District (District) hereby gives notice of its intention to develop revised Rules of Procedure to govern the operations of the District. The proposed rule number is ROP-2026-1. The revised Rules of Procedure will address such areas as the Board of Supervisors, officers and voting, district offices, public information and inspection of records, policies, public meetings, hearings and workshops, rulemaking proceedings, competitive purchase including procedure under the Consultants Competitive Negotiation Act, procedure regarding auditor selection, purchase of insurance, pre-qualification, construction contracts, goods, supplies and materials, maintenance services, contractual services and protests with respect to proceedings, as well as any other area of the general operation of the District. The purpose and effect of the revised Rules of Procedure is to provide for efficient and effective District operations and to ensure compliance with recent changes to Florida law. The specific grant of rulemaking authority for the adoption of the proposed revised Rules of Procedure includes Sections 190.011(5), 190.011(15) and 190.035, Florida Statutes. NOTICE OF RULE DEVELOPMENT BY THE SHINGLE CREEK TRANSIT AND UTILITY COMMUNITY DEVELOPMENT DISTRICT In accordance with Chapters 120 and 190, Florida Statutes, Shingle Creek Transit and Utility Community Development District (the District) hereby gives the public notice of its intention to develop revised Rules of Procedure (the Proposed Rules) to govern the operations of the District. The rule number for the Proposed Rule of the District is 2026-1 The revised Rules of Procedure will address such areas as the Board of Supervisors, officers and voting, district offices, public information and inspection of records, policies, public meetings, hearings and workshops, rulemaking proceedings, competitive purchase including procedure under the Consultants Competitive Negotiation Act, procedure regarding auditor selection, purchase of insurance, pre-qualification, construction contracts, goods, supplies and materials, maintenance services, contractual services and protests with respect to proceedings, as well as any other area of the general operation of the District. The purpose and effect of the revised Rules of Procedure is to provide for efficient and effective district operations and to ensure compliance with recent changes to Florida law. The specific grant of rulemaking authority for the adoption of the proposed revised Rules of Procedure includes Sections 190.011(5), 190.011(15) and 190.035, Florida Statutes. The specific laws implemented in the proposed revised Rules of Procedure include, but are not limited to, [Sections 112.08, 112.3143, 112.31446, 112.3145, 119.07, 119.0701, 120.54, 120.542, 120.5435, 120.56, 120.69, 120.81, 189.053, 189.069, 190.006, 190.007, 190.008, 190.011, 190.033, 190.035, 218.33, 218.391, 255.05, 255.0518, 255.0525, 255.20, 286.0105, 286.011, 286.012, 286.0113, 286.0114, 287.017, 287.055, and 287.084, Florida Statutes. A copy of the proposed revised Rules of Procedure and the related incorporated documents, if any, may be obtained by contacting the District Manager, c/o PFM Management Services LLC, 3501 Quadrangle Boulevard, Suite 270, Orlando, Florida 32817, (407) 723-5900, or waldenj@pfm.com. NOTICE OF PROPOSED ENACTMENT On August 10, 2026, the Orlando City Council will consider proposed ordinance #2026-11, entitled AN ORDINANCE OF THE CITY COUNCIL OF THE CITY OF ORLANDO, FLORIDA, ANNEXING TO THE CORPORATE LIMITS OF THE CITY CERTAIN LAND GENERALLY LOCATED NORTH OF CORRINE DRIVE. EAST OF JANICE AVENUE, SOUTH OF MARBLE AVENUE AND WEST OF E. WINTER PARK ROAD AND COMPRISED OF 0.22 ACRES OF LAND, MORE OR LESS; PROVIDING FOR SEVERABILITY, CORRECTION OF SCRIVENERS ERRORS, AND AN EFFECTIVE DATE. A public hearing on this ordinance will be held during Councils regular meeting beginning at 2:00 p.m., in City Council Chambers, Orlando City Hall, 400 S. Orange Ave., Orlando, Florida. Interested parties may appear at the meeting and be heard with respect to the proposed ordinance. If a person decides to appeal any decision made by Council with respect to any matter considered at the hearing, he or she will need a record of the proceedings, and that, for such purpose, he or she may need to ensure that a verbatim record of the proceedings is made, which record includes testimony and evidence upon which the appeal is based. The proposed ordinance may be inspected by the public at the Office of the City Clerk located on the 2nd floor of Orlando City Hall, 400 S. Orange Ave., Orlando, Florida. Qualified persons with disabilities needing auxiliary aid or service, or other assistance, so they can participate equally in this meeting should contact the Office of the City Clerk at (407) 246-2251 as soon as possible but no later than 48 hours before the meeting.

DECLARATION OF NEXUS DEJURE EXPRESS TRUST Est. October 9, 2023 7:00 AM Schedule A: Trustee Minutes 5-1990 DECLARATION OF NATIONALITY NEXUS DEJURE EXPRESS TRUST (An Irrevocable Express Trust Organization) Other Property Exchange - Non-Real Estate Assets Meeting Convened At 11:15 AM I. INTRODUCTION AND PURPOSE The Sole Trustee, acting on behalf of the Board of Trustees of NEXUS DEJURE EXPRESS TRUST, an Irrevocable Express Trust Organization established on October 9, 2023 at 7:00 AM, acknowledges the filing and recording of this declaration in the Organic Public Record of the Seminole County Public Record in Florida. The County Clerk of Court, herein referenced as an immigration officer of a naturalization court under [8 U.S.C. 1101(7)], bears witness and holds the Settlor's sworn declaration in trust, including that of the Sole Trustee, for presentation before any court established under the Constitution or laws of the United States or any court of record within the Commonwealth in the form that follows: II. STATUTORY NATIONALITY DECLARATION "P. Law 94-241, Article III, Citizenship & Nationality" I, Pollard Jr, Nathaniel d/b/a NATHANIEL POLLARD JR d/b/a NATHANIEL PRINCE, THE DUX OF OKEECHOBEE and forever, hereby declare, state, and claim that I am not, nor have I ever been, a U.S. Citizen or U.S. National. Evidence of this status is documented by my Florida Nation Birth Certificate. Pursuant to the Baby Act and under legal disability, I never consented to enter a Social Security contractual relationship. III. FREE INHABITANT AND DOMICILE STATUS I declare that my legal name is Pollard Jr, Nathaniel d/b/a NATHANIEL POLLARD JR. Let it be publicly known to Immigration Clerks, Homeland Security, Secretaries of State, Supreme Court Judges and Clerks, that I am and shall remain a Free Inhabitant and Native Floridian National pursuant to the 1781 Articles of Confederation, Article IV. I am not a statutory citizen of the United States and am domiciled within the Florida republic of the United States of America (see 2016 GPO Style Manual, pg. 95). DECLARATION OF NEXUS DEJURE EXPRESS TRUST Est. October 9, 2023 7:00 AM Schedule A: Trustee Minutes 5-1990 "Continued" DECLARATION OF NATIONALITY NEXUS DEJURE EXPRESS TRUST (An Irrevocable Express Trust Organization) 1 IV. RENUNCIATION AND STATUS CLARIFICATION I affirm that I have taken an oath and affirmation constituting a declaration of allegiance to a foreign state and that I have formally renounced nationality within the United States. I further affirm that I have not been convicted of any federal or state drug offense, any crime defined under sex tourism" statutes, nor am I subject to any outstanding federal, state, or local felony warrant, court order preventing departure from the United States, or subpoena related to federal prosecution or grand jury investigation, pursuant to U.S. Passport Application DS-11/DS-82 guidance. V. CONTRACTUAL RESCISSION AND NOTICE TAKE JUDICIAL NOTICE: I am not a statutory citizen and make no claim to statutory citizenship created by any State or Federal government. I hereby extinguish, rescind, revoke, cancel, abrogate, annul, nullify, discharge, and void ab initio all signatures belonging to me on all previously executed SS-5 Forms, IRS filings, Superior or District Court Forms, County Municipality Forms, State of Florida DMV documentation, federal and state income tax forms, and any powers of attorney-real or implied-executed under mistake, duress, fraud, or undue influence pursuant to Contract Law. All such forms are hereby extinguished by rescission. DECLARATION OF NEXUS DEJURE EXPRESS TRUST Est. October 9, 2023 7:00 AM Schedule A: Trustee Minutes 5-1990 "Concluded" DECLARATION OF NATIONALITY NEXUS DEJURE EXPRESS TRUST (An Irrevocable Express Trust Organization) VI. REVOCATION OF POWERS OF ATTORNEY I further revoke and nullify all powers of attorney attributed to me from any governmental, quasi-governmental, or colorable agencies or departments created under Article I, Section 8, Clause 17, and/or Article IV, Section 3, Clause 2 of the United States Constitution. VII. AFFIRMATION UNDER PENALTY OF PERJURY I, Pollard Jr, Nathaniel, born on Florida soil within the territory of Okeechobee, declare under penalty of perjury under 28 U.S.C. 1746(1) that I intend to be recognized as a national, but not a citizen, of the United States, pursuant to Public Law 94-241 (March 24, 1976, Article III, Section 302) and that the foregoing statements are true, correct, and complete. PREPARED BY: EXL LEGAL PLLC 12425 28TH ST NO # 200 ST PETERSBURG, FL 33716 NOTICE OF DEFAULT AND FORECLOSURE SALE WHEREAS, on October 10, 2007, a certain Mortgage was executed by JAMES HURLSTON and CELIA HURLSTON as mortgagor in favor of JAMES B. NUTTER & COMPANY as mortgagee, and was recorded on October 23, 2007, in Book 03582, Pages 2053-2063, with CFN 2007190469 in the Office of the Clerk of Circuit Court, Osceola County, Florida; and WHEREAS, the Mortgage was insured by the United States Secretary of Housing and Urban Development (the Secretary) pursuant to the National Housing Act for the purpose of providing single family housing; and WHEREAS, the Mortgage is now owned by the Secretary, pursuant to an assignment dated May 17, 2022, recorded on June 6, 2022, in Book 6227, Page 217, with CFN 2022087681, in the office of the Clerk of Circuit Court, Osceola County, Florida; and WHEREAS, a default has been made in the covenants and conditions of the Mortgage in that the Property ceases to be the principal residence of a Borrower for reasons other than death and the Property is not the principal residence of at least one other Borrower; and WHEREAS, the entire amount delinquent as of June 30, 2026 is $267,143.66; and WHEREAS, by virtue of this default, the Secretary has declared the entire amount of the indebtedness secured by the Mortgage to be immediately due and payable; NOW THEREFORE, pursuant to powers vested in me by the Single Family Mortgage Foreclosure Act of 1994, 12 U.S.C. 3751 et seq., by 24 CFR part 27, subpart B, and by the Secretarys designation of me as Foreclosure Commissioner, recorded on August 12, 2024 in Book 11062, Page 3238, as Instrument No. 2024142828 in the Office of the Clerk of Circuit Court, Pasco County, notice is hereby given that on August 7, 2026 at 11:45 a.m., local time, all real and personal property at or used in connection with the following described premises (Property) will be sold at public auction to the highest bidder: THE FOLLOWING DESCRIBED LAND LOCATED IN THE COUNTY OF OSCEOLA, STATE OF FLORIDA, TO-WIT: LOT 15, BLOCK 1437, POINCIANA, NEIGHBORHOOD 1 NORTH, VILLAGE 1, ACCORDING TO THE PLAT THEREOF, AS RECORDED IN PLAT BOOK 3, PAGES 1-8, OF THE RECORDS OF OSCEOLA COUNTY, FLORIDA. FOR INFORMATIONAL PURPOSES ONLY: THE APN IS SHOWN BY THE COUNTY ASSESSOR AS 25-26-28-6116-1437-0150; SOURCE OF TITLE IS BOOK 0866, PAGE 0308 (RECORDED 1/21/88) Commonly known as: 309 CHIQUITA CT, KISSIMMEE, FL 34758 The sale will be held at the front entrance of the Osceola County Courthouse, 2 Courthouse Sq., Kissimmee, FL 34741. The Secretary of Housing and Urban Development will bid $271,407.25. There will be no proration of taxes, rents or other income or liabilities, except that the purchaser will pay, at or before closing, his prorata share of any real estate taxes that have been paid by the Secretary to the date of the Foreclosure sale. When making their bids, all bidders except the Secretary must submit a deposit totaling $27,140.73 [10% of the Secretarys bid] in the form of a certified check or cashiers check made out to the Secretary of HUD. A deposit need not accompany each oral bid. If the successful bid is oral, a deposit of $27,140.73 must be presented before the bidding is closed. The deposit is nonrefundable. The remainder of the purchase price must be delivered within 30 days of the sale or at such other time as the Secretary may determine for good cause shown, time being of the essence. This amount, like the bid deposits, must be delivered in the form of a certified or cashiers check. If the Secretary is the highest bidder, he need not pay the bid amount in cash. The successful bidder will pay all conveying fees, all real estate and other taxes that are due on or after the delivery date of the remainder of the payment and all other costs associated with the transfer of title. At the conclusion of the sale, the deposits of the unsuccessful bidders will be returned to them. The Secretary may grant an extension of time within which to deliver the remainder of the payment. All extensions will be for 15-day increments for a fee of $500.00, paid in advance. The extension fee shall be in the form of a certified or cashiers check made payable to the Secretary of HUD. If the high bidder closes the sale prior to the expiration of any extension period, the unused portion of the extension fee shall be applied toward the amount due. If the high bidder is unable to close the sale within the required period, or within any extensions of time granted by the Secretary, the high bidder may be required to forfeit the cash deposit or, at the election of the foreclosure commissioner after consultation with the HUD representative, will be liable to HUD for any costs incurred as a result of such failure. The Commissioner may, at the direction of the HUD representative, offer the property to the second highest bidder for an amount equal to the highest price offered by that bidder. There is no right of redemption, or right of possession based upon a right of redemption, in the mortgagor or others subsequent to a foreclosure completed pursuant to the Act. Therefore, the Foreclosure Commissioner will issue a Deed to the purchaser(s) upon receipt of the entire purchase price in accordance with the terms of the sale as provided herein. HUD does not guarantee that the property will be vacant. The scheduled foreclosure sale shall be cancelled or adjourned if it is established, by documented written application of the mortgagor to the Foreclosure Commissioner not less than 3 days before the date of sale, or otherwise, that the default or defaults upon which the foreclosure is based did not exist at the time of service of this notice of default and foreclosure sale, or all amounts due under the mortgage agreement are tendered to the Foreclosure Commissioner, in the form of a certified or cashiers check payable to the Secretary of HUD, before public auction of the property is completed. The amount that must be paid if the mortgage is to be reinstated prior to the scheduled sale is $267,143.66 as of August 7, 2026, plus all other amounts that would be due under the mortgage agreement if payments under the mortgage had not been accelerated, advertising costs and postage expenses incurred in giving notice, mileage by the most reasonable road distance for posting notices and for the Foreclosure Commissioners attendance at the sale, reasonable and customary costs incurred for title and lien record searches, the necessary out-of-pocket costs incurred by the Foreclosure Commissioner for recording documents, a commission for the Foreclosure Commissioner, and all other costs incurred in connection with the foreclosure prior to reinstatement. NOTICE OF REGULAR MONTHLY MEETINGS OF ORANGE COUNTY INDUSTRIAL DEVELOPMENT AUTHORITY Regular monthly meetings of the Orange County Industrial Development Authority (the Authority) normally are held at 2:00 p.m., local time, on the third Tuesday of each month. For the balance of 2026 and the first seven months of 2027, such regular monthly meetings will be on the following dates: August 18, 2026 September 15, 2026 October 20, 2026 November 17, 2026 December 15, 2026 January 19, 2027 February 16, 2027 March 16, 2027 April 20, 2027 May 18, 2027 June 15, 2027 July 20, 2027 All such regular monthly meetings will be held in the Orange County Administration Center at 201 South Rosalind, Orlando, Florida 32801. Such meetings usually are held on the third floor in Legal Conference Room A. If for any reason the Legal Conference Room A is not available for a particular meeting, the meeting may be held in another room in the Orange County Administration Center that will be posted in such building. ALL OF THESE MEETINGS ARE OPEN TO THE PUBLIC. A draft agenda showing known matters to be discussed at each meeting normally is posted on Orange Countys website approximately 10 days before the scheduled date of the meeting, but the Authority also may consider any other business properly coming before any meeting of the Authority. In months in which the Authority has no business to conduct, the scheduled monthly meeting may be canceled. Notice of the cancellation of a scheduled monthly meeting normally is posted on Orange Countys website approximately 10 days before the scheduled meeting date. Persons are advised that, if they decide to appeal any decision made at any meeting of the Authority, they will need a record of the proceedings, and, for such purpose, they may need to insure that a verbatim record of the proceedings is made, which record includes the testimony and evidence upon which the appeal is to be based. Section 286.0105, Florida Statutes, states that if a person decides to appeal any decision made by a board, agency, or commission with respect to any matter considered at a meeting or hearing, he or she will need a record of the proceedings, and that, for such purpose, he or she may need to ensure that a verbatim record of the proceedings is made, which record includes the testimony and evidence upon which the appeal is to be based. La Seccin 286.0105 de los Estatutos de Florida establece que si una persona decide apelar cualquier decisin tomada por una junta, agencia o comisin con respecto a cualquier asunto considerado en una reunin o audiencia, necesitar un registro de los procedimientos, y que , para tal efecto, podr necesitar asegurarse de que se levante un acta literal delproceso, en la que se incluyan los testimonios y pruebas en que se basar la apelacin. Atik 286.0105, Lwa Florid, deklare ke si yon moun deside depoze yon apl kont desizyon yon komite, ajans, oswa yon komisyon te pran konsnan yon sij ki te diskite nan yon reyinyon oswa yon odyans, li pral bezwen yon rap sou sa ki te diskite yo, e ke, pou rezon sa a, li ka bezwen asire ke li gen yon rap egzak nan pwosedi ki te ft la, enkli temwayaj ak prv ke apl la dwe baze a. Orange County does not discriminate on the basis of race, color, national origin, sex, age, religion, disability or family status. Those with questions or concerns about nondiscrimination, those requiring special assistance under the Americans with Disabilities Act (ADA), and those requiring language assistance (free of charge) should contact the Title VI/Nondiscrimination Coordinator at access@ocfl.net or by calling 3-1-1 (407-836-3111). If you are hearing or speech impaired, you may reach the phone numbers above by dialing 711. El Condado de Orange no discrimina por motivos de raza, color, origen nacional, sexo, edad, religin, discapacidad o situacin familiar. Aquellos que tengan preguntas o inquietudes sabre la no discriminacin, aquellos que requieran asistencia especial segn la Ley de Estadounidenses con Discapacidades (ADA) y aquellos que requieran asistencia lingstica (gratuita) deben comunicarse con el Coordinador de No Discriminacin/Ttulo VI en access@ocfl.net o llamando 3-1-1 (407-836- 3111). Si tiene problemas de audicin o del habla, puede comunicarse con los nmeros de telfono anteriores marcando 711. Komin Oranj pa f diskriminasyon sou baz ras, koul, orijin nasyonal, sks, laj, relijyon, andikap oswa sitiyasyon familyal. Moun ki gen kesyon oswa enkyetid konsnan non-diskriminasyon, moun ki bezwen asistans espesyal dapr Lwa Ameriken pou Andikape yo (ADA), ak moun ki bezwen d nan lang (gratis) dwe kontakte Kowdonat Tit VI/Non-diskriminasyon an nan access@ocfl.net oswa yo ka rele 3-1-1 (407-836-3111). Si w soud oswa bb, ou ka rele nimewo telefn ki anwo, peze 711. This notice is given pursuant to Section 189.015 and Chapter 286, Florida Statutes, in order to provide reasonable public notice of the Authoritys regular meetings. CITY OF APOPKA PUBLIC HEARING NOTICE The following ordinance will be read at First Reading on Wednesday, August 5, 2026 at 1:30 p.m. and will be held over for a Second Reading and adoption on Wednesday, August 19, 2026 at 6:00 p.m. or as soon thereafter as possible at the City Council meeting in the Apopka City Hall Council Chambers. ORDINANCE NO. 3168 AN ORDINANCE OF THE CITY COMMISSION OF THE CITY OF APOPKA, FLORIDA; AMENDING SECTION 2.3, ADVISORY AND DECISION-MAKING BODIES AND PERSONS, OF ARTICLE 2 OF THE CITYS LAND DEVELOPMENT CODE; AMENDING THE PROCEDURES FOR THE APPOINTMENT OF MEMBERS TO THE CITY OF APOPKA PLANNING COMMISSION; ESTABLISHING ELIGIBILITY AND APPLICATION REQUIREMENTS; PROVIDING FOR OATH OF OFFICE AND FINANCIAL DISCLOSURE REQUIREMENTS; PROVIDING FOR CONFLICTS, SEVERABILITY, SCRIVENERS ERRORS, CODIFICATION, AND AN EFFECTIVE DATE. Interested parties may appear at this meeting and be heard with respect to the proposed ordinance. The proposed ordinance is available in the City Clerks office for inspection. Please be advised that, under State law, if you decide to appeal a decision made with respect to this matter, you will need a record of the proceedings and may need to ensure that a verbatim record is made, which record includes the testimony and evidence upon which the appeal is to be based. In accordance with the Americans with Disabilities Act (ADA), persons with disabilities needing assistance to participate in any of these proceedings should contact the City Clerks Office at 120 East Main Street, Apopka FL 32703, Telephone: 407-703-1704, no less than 48 hours prior to the proceeding. 109967 7/29/2026 8/05/2026

INVITATION TO BID CENTRAL FLORIDA EXPRESSWAY AUTHORITY (CFX) ORLANDO, FLORIDA Sealed Electronic Bids for the work generally described below will be received on September 01, 2026, until 1:30p.m., Orlando local time, as shown on the CFX website. The CFX website will serve as the official instrument to determine when a bid is received. A submittal that is received after the date/time specified will be rejected. Rejection of a submittal under those circumstances shall not be the basis of a protest pursuant to the CFX Procedure for Resolution of Protests. Bid opening at CFXs Headquarters Building located at 4974 ORL Tower Road, Orlando, FL. 32807, shall be held September 02, 2026, at 9:00a.m. Such bid opening will additionally be accessible by online stream by use of the web-conference information provided in the full version of this invitation to bid. SYSTEMWIDE SIGNING IMPROVEMENTS SOLICITATION NO. 270702, PROJECT NO. 599-579 The work consists of providing all labor, materials, equipment and incidentals necessary for the installation of wrong-way driving (WWD) equipment at 46 interchanges located along the CFX roadway system. Work involved will include installation of single post signs, replacement of sign panels, installation of minor striping and all other items included in the construction plans. For a full version of the invitation for this project including additional information regarding availability of the Bidding Documents, pre-qualification requirements, pre-bid meeting, SBE participation, protest procedure, and bonding requirements refer to CFXs web site at www.CFXWay.com. Published: July 28, 2026

NOTICE OF CHANGE IN CONTROL Sharon M. France, Daytona Beach, Florida; Jennifer F. Bates, Winter Park, Florida; Amy L. France, Orlando, Florida and Jamison C. France, Ormond Beach, Florida intend to apply to the Federal Reserve Board for permission to retain 25 percent or more of the shares and thereby control of Commerce National Bankshares of Florida, Inc. Commerce National Bankshares of Florida, Inc. controls Commerce Bank & Trust, Winter Park, Florida as a subsidiary bank. The Federal Reserve considers a number of factors in deciding whether to approve the notice. You are invited to submit comments in writing on this notice to the Federal Reserve Bank of Atlanta, 1000 Peachtree Street N.E., Atlanta, Georgia 30309-4470. The comment period will not end before August 18, 2026, and may be somewhat longer. The Board's procedures for processing applications may be found at 12 C.F.R. Part 262.25. To obtain a copy of the Federal Reserve Board's procedures, or if you need more information about how to submit your comments on the notice, contact Erien Terry, Assistant Vice President, at (404) 384-0267. The Federal Reserve will consider your comments on the notice if they are received in writing by the Reserve Bank on or before the last day of the comment period.
